import { getTasksForLevel } from './levels/registry.js';
import { getWrongPhrases } from './data/wrongPhrases.js';
import { setupKeyboard } from './components/keyboard.js';
import { setupModals } from './components/modals.js';
import { openAdminPanel } from './components/admin.js';
import { renderCategoryFilters } from './components/categoryFilters.js';
import { renderTimerStatusBar } from './components/timerStatusBar.js';
import { isIntroHidden, showProjectPresentation } from './components/onboarding.js';
import { openSettingsModal } from './components/settingsModal.js';
import {
  recordDailyProgress,
  recordAnswerAttempt,
  getLocalCounters,
  saveLocalCounters
} from './utils/statsTracker.js';
import {
  speakEnglishWord,
  speakErrorMessage,
  stopErrorSpeech,
  isEnglishPhraseSpeechEnabled
} from './utils/speechFeedback.js';
import { calculateStyleLevel, calculateElementLevel, gameConfig } from './config/gameConfig.js';
import { applyStyleLevelToBody } from './utils/styleTheme.js';
import { applyDynamicSplashScreen } from './utils/pwaBranding.js';
import {
  initViewportAdaptation,
  syncViewportMetrics,
  applyCustomKeyboardInputGuards,
  setCustomInputValueWithCaret
} from './utils/platformCompat.js';
import {
  checkAndApplyRemoteVersion,
  startRemoteVersionWatcher,
  onUpdateAvailable,
  applyPendingAppUpdate
} from './utils/appUpdate.js';
import {
  shareProjectContent,
  buildProjectShareText,
  buildLevelUpShareText,
  SHARE_APP_URL
} from './utils/share.js';
import { buildGuestResetState } from './utils/sessionReset.js';

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const app = document.querySelector('#app');
  initViewportAdaptation();
  applyDynamicSplashScreen().catch(() => {});

  // Счётчики ТОЛЬКО для текущей сессии (начинаются с 0 при открытии приложения)
  let sessionCorrectAnswers = 0;
  let sessionIncorrectAnswers = 0;

  // Накопленные счётчики для уровней эволюции и синхронизации со статистикой/Firestore
  const initialCounters = getLocalCounters(null);
  let totalCorrectAnswers = initialCounters.correct;
  let totalIncorrectAnswers = initialCounters.incorrect;

  let sessionIndex = 0;
  let currentLevel = 1;
  let activeCategories = ['statement'];
  let consecutiveErrors = 0;
  let currentTask = null;
  let isEnglishToRussian = false;
  let currentInput = "";
  let lastFeedback = "";
  let lastFeedbackColor = "";
  let isAdvancingAfterSpeech = false;
  let advanceTimeoutId = null;

  let secondsElapsed = 0;
  let lastSyncedSeconds = 0;
  let timerInterval = null;
  let currentUser = null;
  let isAdminUser = false;
  let userUnsubscribe = null;
  let deferredPrompt = null;
  let hasShownStartupPresentation = false;
  let availableUpdateVersion = null;

  let isTestMode = false;
  let testQuestionsAnswered = 0;
  let testCorrectCount = 0;
  let testSecondsLeft = gameConfig.testTimeSeconds;
  let testTimerInterval = null;

  let isSoundEnabled = localStorage.getItem('rkai_sound_enabled') !== 'false';
  let isEnglishPhraseSpeechActive = isEnglishPhraseSpeechEnabled();

  const MAX_INPUT_LENGTH = 35;

  // Прогреваем список голосов браузера, если он подгружается асинхронно
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) {
    window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
    window.speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged = () => {
      window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
    };
  }

  function resetViewportPosition() {
    syncViewportMetrics();
    try {
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, left: 0, behavior: 'instant' });
    } catch (_) {
      window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    }
    if (document.scrollingElement) {
      document.scrollingElement.scrollTop = 0;
      document.scrollingElement.scrollLeft = 0;
    }
    document.documentElement.scrollTop = 0;
    document.body.scrollTop = 0;
    if (app) {
      app.scrollTop = 0;
      app.scrollLeft = 0;
    }
  }

  function preserveInputFocusAndCaret(inputEl, fallbackCaret = null) {
    if (!inputEl || isEnglishToRussian || isAdvancingAfterSpeech) return;
    applyCustomKeyboardInputGuards(inputEl);
    const caretPos = typeof fallbackCaret === 'number'
      ? Math.max(0, Math.min(fallbackCaret, inputEl.value.length))
      : inputEl.value.length;

    if (!inputEl.readOnly) {
      inputEl.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }
    try {
      inputEl.selectionStart = inputEl.selectionEnd = caretPos;
    } catch (_) {}
    resetViewportPosition();
  }

  function clearPendingAdvance() {
    if (advanceTimeoutId) {
      clearTimeout(advanceTimeoutId);
      advanceTimeoutId = null;
    }
    isAdvancingAfterSpeech = false;
  }

  function stopTestTimerIfRunning() {
    if (testTimerInterval) {
      clearInterval(testTimerInterval);
      testTimerInterval = null;
    }
  }

  function getAvailableCategoriesForLevel(lvl) {
    if (lvl >= 3) return ['question', 'statement', 'negative'];
    if (lvl === 2) return ['statement', 'negative'];
    return ['statement'];
  }

  function syncCategoriesWithLevel(lvl) {
    activeCategories = getAvailableCategoriesForLevel(lvl);
    resetTaskPools();
  }

  function resetAllLevelsAndCountersForLogout() {
    clearPendingAdvance();
    stopErrorSpeech();
    stopTestTimerIfRunning();

    const resetState = buildGuestResetState({
      defaultTestSeconds: gameConfig.testTimeSeconds,
      resetGuestStorage: true
    });

    currentUser = resetState.currentUser;
    isAdminUser = resetState.isAdminUser;
    currentLevel = resetState.currentLevel;
    sessionCorrectAnswers = resetState.sessionCorrectAnswers;
    sessionIncorrectAnswers = resetState.sessionIncorrectAnswers;
    totalCorrectAnswers = resetState.totalCorrectAnswers;
    totalIncorrectAnswers = resetState.totalIncorrectAnswers;
    sessionIndex = resetState.sessionIndex;
    consecutiveErrors = resetState.consecutiveErrors;
    currentInput = resetState.currentInput;
    lastFeedback = resetState.lastFeedback;
    lastFeedbackColor = resetState.lastFeedbackColor;
    secondsElapsed = resetState.secondsElapsed;
    lastSyncedSeconds = resetState.lastSyncedSeconds;
    isTestMode = resetState.isTestMode;
    testQuestionsAnswered = resetState.testQuestionsAnswered;
    testCorrectCount = resetState.testCorrectCount;
    testSecondsLeft = resetState.testSecondsLeft;
    activeCategories = resetState.activeCategories;

    updateStyleLevel();
    pickNewTask();
  }

  function handleToggleCategory(cat) {
    if (isTestMode) return;
    clearPendingAdvance();
    stopErrorSpeech();
    const available = getAvailableCategoriesForLevel(currentLevel);

    if (activeCategories.includes(cat)) {
      const next = activeCategories.filter(c => c !== cat);
      activeCategories = next.length > 0 ? next : [...available];
    } else {
      activeCategories = [...activeCategories, cat];
    }

    resetTaskPools();
    currentInput = "";
    lastFeedback = "";
    pickNewTask();
    renderTrainer();
  }

  const audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
  function playSound(type) {
    if (!isSoundEnabled) return;
    if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
      audioCtx.resume();
    }
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);

    if (type === 'success') {
      osc.type = 'sine';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(587.33, audioCtx.currentTime);
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, audioCtx.currentTime + 0.08);
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + 0.25);
      osc.start();
      osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + 0.25);
    } else if (type === 'error') {
      osc.type = 'sawtooth';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(150, audioCtx.currentTime);
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(100, audioCtx.currentTime + 0.1);
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + 0.2);
      osc.start();
      osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + 0.2);
    }
  }

  function speakCorrectAnswerAndWait(spokenText, onDone) {
    let finished = false;
    const finishOnce = () => {
      if (finished) return;
      finished = true;
      if (advanceTimeoutId) {
        clearTimeout(advanceTimeoutId);
        advanceTimeoutId = null;
      }
      if (typeof onDone === 'function') {
        onDone();
      }
    };

    if (!isEnglishPhraseSpeechActive || !spokenText || typeof window === 'undefined' || !('speechSynthesis' in window)) {
      finishOnce();
      return;
    }

    const estimatedMs = Math.min(3500, Math.max(1400, spokenText.length * 95 + 600));
    advanceTimeoutId = setTimeout(finishOnce, estimatedMs);

    speakEnglishWord(spokenText, {
      rate: 0.92,
      pitch: 1.0,
      onEnd: () => {
        setTimeout(finishOnce, 180);
      }
    });
  }

  if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
    window.addEventListener('load', () => {
      navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js').catch(() => {});
    });
  }

  // Подписка на мягкое уведомление о новой версии без сдвига экрана и потери фокуса
  onUpdateAvailable((ver) => {
    const nextVer = ver || null;
    if (availableUpdateVersion === nextVer) return;
    const wasBannerShown = Boolean(availableUpdateVersion);
    availableUpdateVersion = nextVer;

    const prevInput = document.getElementById('answer-display');
    const savedCaret = prevInput ? prevInput.selectionStart : null;

    renderTrainer();

    if (wasBannerShown && !nextVer) {
      resetViewportPosition();
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        resetViewportPosition();
        const nextInput = document.getElementById('answer-display');
        preserveInputFocusAndCaret(nextInput, savedCaret);
      });
      return;
    }

    const nextInput = document.getElementById('answer-display');
    preserveInputFocusAndCaret(nextInput, savedCaret);
  });

  // Проверка доступной версии при старте и при возврате в приложение
  checkAndApplyRemoteVersion(true);
  startRemoteVersionWatcher();

  window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    deferredPrompt = e;
    renderTrainer();
  });

  function flushSessionTime() {
    const deltaSeconds = secondsElapsed - lastSyncedSeconds;
    if (deltaSeconds <= 0) return 0;
    lastSyncedSeconds = secondsElapsed;

    recordDailyProgress({ user: currentUser, secondsDelta: deltaSeconds });

    if (currentUser) {
      const userRef = doc(db, "users", currentUser.uid);
      updateDoc(userRef, {
        totalTimeSpent: increment(deltaSeconds),
        lastActive: serverTimestamp()
      }).catch(() => {});
    }
    return deltaSeconds;
  }

  window.addEventListener('beforeunload', () => {
    clearPendingAdvance();
    stopErrorSpeech();
    flushSessionTime();
  });

  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
    if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') {
      clearPendingAdvance();
      stopErrorSpeech();
      flushSessionTime();
    } else if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
      resetViewportPosition();
      checkAndApplyRemoteVersion(true);
    }
  });

  function updateStyleLevel() {
    const styleLevel = calculateStyleLevel(totalCorrectAnswers);
    applyStyleLevelToBody(styleLevel);
    return styleLevel;
  }

  function getWrongPhrase(errorsCount) {
    const phrases = getWrongPhrases();
    const lvl = Math.min(Math.max(errorsCount, 1), 5);
    const list = phrases[lvl] || phrases[1];
    return list[Math.floor(Math.random() * list.length)];
  }

  async function syncUserProgressToCloud() {
    updateStyleLevel();
    saveLocalCounters(currentUser, {
      correct: totalCorrectAnswers,
      incorrect: totalIncorrectAnswers
    });

    const deltaSeconds = secondsElapsed - lastSyncedSeconds;
    if (deltaSeconds > 0) {
      lastSyncedSeconds = secondsElapsed;
      await recordDailyProgress({
        user: currentUser,
        tasksDelta: 0,
        secondsDelta: deltaSeconds
      });
    }

    if (!currentUser) return;
    try {
      const userRef = doc(db, "users", currentUser.uid);

      await setDoc(userRef, {
        uid: currentUser.uid,
        email: currentUser.email || "anonymous",
        displayName: currentUser.displayName || "",
        photoURL: currentUser.photoURL || "",
        totalCorrect: totalCorrectAnswers,
        totalIncorrect: totalIncorrectAnswers,
        currentLessonLevel: currentLevel,
        totalTimeSpent: increment(deltaSeconds > 0 ? deltaSeconds : 0),
        lastActive: serverTimestamp()
      }, { merge: true });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Ошибка сохранения данных:", error);
    }
  }

  async function initUserProfile(user) {
    if (!user) return;
    try {
      const userRef = doc(db, "users", user.uid);
      const docSnap = await getDoc(userRef);

      if (!docSnap.exists()) {
        const localCounters = getLocalCounters(user);
        totalCorrectAnswers = localCounters.correct;
        totalIncorrectAnswers = localCounters.incorrect;
        syncCategoriesWithLevel(1);
        await setDoc(userRef, {
          uid: user.uid,
          email: user.email || "anonymous",
          displayName: user.displayName || "",
          photoURL: user.photoURL || "",
          isAdmin: false,
          currentLessonLevel: 1,
          totalCorrect: totalCorrectAnswers,
          totalIncorrect: totalIncorrectAnswers,
          visitCount: 1,
          totalTimeSpent: 0,
          lastLogin: serverTimestamp()
        });
      } else {
        const data = docSnap.data();
        totalCorrectAnswers = Number(data.totalCorrect) || 0;
        totalIncorrectAnswers = Number(data.totalIncorrect) || 0;
        saveLocalCounters(user, {
          correct: totalCorrectAnswers,
          incorrect: totalIncorrectAnswers
        });

        currentLevel = data.currentLessonLevel || 1;
        syncCategoriesWithLevel(currentLevel);
        pickNewTask();

        updateStyleLevel();
        await setDoc(userRef, {
          lastLogin: serverTimestamp(),
          visitCount: increment(1),
          totalCorrect: totalCorrectAnswers,
          totalIncorrect: totalIncorrectAnswers
        }, { merge: true });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Ошибка инициализации профиля:", error);
    }
  }

  onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
    if (userUnsubscribe) {
      userUnsubscribe();
      userUnsubscribe = null;
    }

    if (user) {
      currentUser = user;
      await initUserProfile(user);

      const userRef = doc(db, "users", user.uid);
      userUnsubscribe = onSnapshot(userRef, (docSnap) => {
        if (docSnap.exists()) {
          const data = docSnap.data();
          const newAdminStatus = !!data.isAdmin;
          if (isAdminUser !== newAdminStatus) {
            isAdminUser = newAdminStatus;
            renderTrainer();
          }
        }
      });
    } else {
      resetAllLevelsAndCountersForLogout();
    }
    renderTrainer();
  });

  function getCurrentTasksForLevel(lvl) {
    return getTasksForLevel(lvl);
  }

  function pickNewTask() {
    const tasks = getCurrentTasksForLevel(currentLevel);
    const categoriesToUse = isTestMode ? getAvailableCategoriesForLevel(currentLevel) : activeCategories;
    const result = getCyclicTask(sessionIndex, tasks, categoriesToUse);
    currentTask = result.task;
    isEnglishToRussian = result.isEnglishToRussian;
  }

  function formatTime(totalSeconds) {
    const mins = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60);
    const secs = totalSeconds % 60;
    return `${mins}:${secs < 10 ? '0' : ''}${secs}`;
  }

  function startTimer() {
    if (timerInterval) return;
    timerInterval = setInterval(() => {
      secondsElapsed++;
      const timerEl = document.getElementById('timer-display');
      const elementLevel = calculateElementLevel(totalCorrectAnswers);
      if (timerEl && elementLevel >= 3 && !isTestMode) {
        timerEl.textContent = `${formatTime(secondsElapsed)}`;
      }
    }, 1000);
  }

  function startTestMode() {
    clearPendingAdvance();
    stopErrorSpeech();
    stopTestTimerIfRunning();
    isTestMode = true;
    sessionIndex = 0;
    currentInput = "";
    testQuestionsAnswered = 0;
    testCorrectCount = 0;
    testSecondsLeft = gameConfig.testTimeSeconds;
    resetTaskPools();
    pickNewTask();
    renderTrainer();

    testTimerInterval = setInterval(() => {
      testSecondsLeft--;
      const testTimerEl = document.getElementById('test-timer-display');
      if (testTimerEl) {
        testTimerEl.textContent = `Осталось времени: ${formatTime(testSecondsLeft)}`;
      }

      if (testSecondsLeft <= 0) {
        finishTest(false);
      }
    }, 1000);
  }

  function finishTest(passed) {
    clearPendingAdvance();
    stopErrorSpeech();
    stopTestTimerIfRunning();
    isTestMode = false;
    sessionIndex = 0;
    currentInput = "";

    const totalQ = gameConfig.testQuestionCount;
    const requiredCorrect = Math.ceil(totalQ * 0.9);

    if (passed && testCorrectCount >= requiredCorrect) {
      const unlockedLevel = currentLevel + 1;
      currentLevel = unlockedLevel;
      syncCategoriesWithLevel(currentLevel);
      syncUserProgressToCloud();

      if (confirm(`Поздравляем! Вы успешно прошли тест (${testCorrectCount}/${totalQ}) и переходите на Уровень ${unlockedLevel}!\n\nПоделиться результатом с друзьями?`)) {
        shareProjectContent({
          title: `Уровень ${unlockedLevel} в Pareto English`,
          text: buildLevelUpShareText(unlockedLevel),
          url: SHARE_APP_URL
        });
      }
    } else {
      alert(`Время вышло или недостаточно правильных ответов (${testCorrectCount}/${totalQ}, нужно >= ${requiredCorrect}). Вы остаетесь на текущем уровне, тренируйтесь дальше!`);
      resetTaskPools();
    }
    pickNewTask();
    renderTrainer();
  }

  async function handleAnswerResult(isCorrect, spokenEnglishText = "") {
    if (isAdvancingAfterSpeech) return;

    const elementLevel = calculateElementLevel(totalCorrectAnswers);

    if (isTestMode) {
      testQuestionsAnswered++;
      if (isCorrect) {
        stopErrorSpeech();
        testCorrectCount++;
        sessionCorrectAnswers++;
        const updatedCounters = await recordAnswerAttempt({
          user: currentUser,
          isCorrect: true,
          countTowardCompletedTasks: true
        });
        totalCorrectAnswers = updatedCounters.correct;
        totalIncorrectAnswers = updatedCounters.incorrect;
        await syncUserProgressToCloud();
        if (elementLevel >= 5) playSound('success');
      } else {
        sessionIncorrectAnswers++;
        const updatedCounters = await recordAnswerAttempt({
          user: currentUser,
          isCorrect: false,
          countTowardCompletedTasks: false
        });
        totalCorrectAnswers = updatedCounters.correct;
        totalIncorrectAnswers = updatedCounters.incorrect;
        await syncUserProgressToCloud();
        if (elementLevel >= 5) playSound('error');
      }

      const totalQ = gameConfig.testQuestionCount;
      const requiredCorrect = Math.ceil(totalQ * 0.9);

      if (testQuestionsAnswered >= totalQ) {
        if (testCorrectCount >= requiredCorrect) {
          finishTest(true);
        } else {
          finishTest(false);
        }
        return;
      }
      sessionIndex++;
      currentInput = "";
      pickNewTask();
      renderTrainer();
      return;
    }

    if (isCorrect) {
      stopErrorSpeech();
      sessionCorrectAnswers++;
    } else {
      sessionIncorrectAnswers++;
    }

    const updatedCounters = await recordAnswerAttempt({
      user: currentUser,
      isCorrect,
      countTowardCompletedTasks: true
    });
    totalCorrectAnswers = updatedCounters.correct;
    totalIncorrectAnswers = updatedCounters.incorrect;

    if (isCorrect) {
      consecutiveErrors = 0;

      if (elementLevel >= 1) {
        lastFeedback = "Правильно";
        lastFeedbackColor = "#22c55e";
      }

      const shouldSpeakEnglish =
        isEnglishPhraseSpeechActive &&
        elementLevel >= 5 &&
        !isEnglishToRussian &&
        Boolean(spokenEnglishText);

      if (shouldSpeakEnglish) {
        isAdvancingAfterSpeech = true;
        renderTrainer();

        if (elementLevel >= 6) {
          const inputEl = document.getElementById('answer-display');
          if (inputEl) {
            inputEl.classList.add('success-flash');
          }
        }

        await syncUserProgressToCloud();

        speakCorrectAnswerAndWait(spokenEnglishText, () => {
          isAdvancingAfterSpeech = false;
          sessionIndex++;
          currentInput = "";
          pickNewTask();
          renderTrainer();
        });
        return;
      }

      if (elementLevel >= 5) {
        playSound('success');
      }

      if (elementLevel >= 6 && !isEnglishToRussian) {
        const inputEl = document.getElementById('answer-display');
        if (inputEl) {
          inputEl.classList.add('success-flash');
        }

        sessionIndex++;
        currentInput = "";

        setTimeout(() => {
          pickNewTask();
          renderTrainer();
        }, 300);

        await syncUserProgressToCloud();
        return;
      }

      sessionIndex++;
      currentInput = "";
      pickNewTask();
      await syncUserProgressToCloud();
      renderTrainer();
    } else {
      consecutiveErrors++;

      if (elementLevel >= 5) {
        playSound('error');
      }

      if (elementLevel >= 1) {
        const phrase = getWrongPhrase(consecutiveErrors);
        lastFeedback = phrase;
        lastFeedbackColor = "#ef4444";

        if (consecutiveErrors > 1) {
          speakErrorMessage(phrase, { enabled: isSoundEnabled });
        }
      }

      if (elementLevel >= 6 && !isEnglishToRussian) {
        const inputEl = document.getElementById('answer-display');
        if (inputEl) {
          inputEl.classList.add('shake-effect');
          setTimeout(() => {
            inputEl.classList.remove('shake-effect');
          }, 400);
        }
      }

      await syncUserProgressToCloud();
      renderTrainer();
    }
  }

  function maybeShowStartupPresentation() {
    if (hasShownStartupPresentation) return;
    hasShownStartupPresentation = true;

    if (!isIntroHidden()) {
      showProjectPresentation({
        onClose: () => {
          const inputEl = document.getElementById('answer-display');
          preserveInputFocusAndCaret(inputEl);
        }
      });
    }
  }

  function renderTrainer() {
    if (!currentTask) {
      pickNewTask();
    }

    syncViewportMetrics();
    updateStyleLevel();
    const elementLevel = calculateElementLevel(totalCorrectAnswers);

    const showFeedback = elementLevel >= 1;
    const showCounters = elementLevel >= 2;
    const showShareButton = !isTestMode && showCounters;
    const showTimer = elementLevel >= 3 && !isTestMode;
    const showTestButton = elementLevel >= 4;
    const showSoundToggle = elementLevel >= 5;
    const showSettingsButton = Boolean(currentUser) && elementLevel >= 7;
    const showSoftUpdateBanner = Boolean(availableUpdateVersion) && !isTestMode;

    app.innerHTML = `
      <div class="app-header">
        ${showSoftUpdateBanner ? `
          <div id="soft-update-banner" style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; gap: 8px; padding: 6px 10px; margin-bottom: var(--ui-gap); border-radius: 8px; background: var(--accent-bg); border: 1px solid var(--accent); font-size: 12px;">
            <span style="font-weight: 600; text-align: left; line-height: 1.25;">Доступна версия v${availableUpdateVersion}</span>
            <button type="button" id="apply-soft-update-btn" style="padding: 4px 10px; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 700; cursor: pointer; background: var(--accent); color: #fff; border: none; flex-shrink: 0;">Обновить</button>
          </div>
        ` : ''}

        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; font-size: 13px; margin-bottom: var(--ui-gap); gap: 6px;">
          <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; min-width: 0;">
            <span style="font-weight: 800; color: var(--accent); background: var(--accent-bg); padding: 2px 7px; border-radius: 6px; border: 1px solid var(--accent); white-space: nowrap; font-size: 12px;">Pareto English</span>
            <span style="white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;">
              ${currentUser ? (currentUser.displayName || currentUser.email) : 'Гость'} · Ур. ${currentLevel}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; flex-shrink: 0;">
            ${showShareButton ? `
              <button id="share-top-btn" title="Поделиться" aria-label="Поделиться" style="background: none; border: none; cursor: pointer; font-size: 14px; color: inherit; padding: 2px; line-height: 1;">📤</button>
            ` : ''}
            ${currentUser ? `
              <button id="logout-btn" style="background: none; border: none; text-decoration: underline; cursor: pointer; font-size: 13px; color: inherit; padding: 2px;">Выйти</button>
            ` : `
              <button id="login-trigger-btn" style="background: none; border: none; text-decoration: underline; cursor: pointer; font-size: 13px; color: inherit; padding: 2px;">Войти</button>
            `}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div id="timer-status-bar-container"></div>

        ${showTestButton && !isTestMode ? `
          <div style="margin-bottom: var(--ui-gap);">
            <button id="start-test-btn" style="width: 100%; padding: clamp(7px, 1.2dvh, 10px); font-weight: bold; font-size: clamp(13px, 2dvh, 15px); cursor: pointer; border-radius: 8px;">Перейти на ${currentLevel + 1} уровень (тест)</button>
          </div>
        ` : ''}

        <div id="category-filters-container"></div>

        ${isTestMode ? `
          <div style="margin-bottom: var(--ui-gap); background: rgba(170,59,255,0.1); padding: 6px 10px; border: 1px solid var(--accent); border-radius: 8px;">
            <div style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 13px;">РЕЖИМ ТЕСТИРОВАНИЯ (${testQuestionsAnswered}/${gameConfig.testQuestionCount})</div>
            <div id="test-timer-display" style="font-size: 14px; color: #ef4444; font-weight: bold;">Осталось времени: ${formatTime(testSecondsLeft)}</div>
          </div>
        ` : ''}

        ${deferredPrompt ? `
          <div style="margin-bottom: var(--ui-gap);">
            <button id="install-pwa-btn" style="width: 100%; padding: 8px; font-size: 14px; font-weight: bold; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 8px;">📲 Установить приложение</button>
          </div>
        ` : ''}

        ${isAdminUser ? `
          <div style="margin-bottom: var(--ui-gap); text-align: center;">
            <button id="admin-panel-btn" style="padding: 5px 12px; font-size: 12px; font-weight: bold; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 6px;">📊 Админ-панель</button>
          </div>
        ` : ''}
      </div>

      <div class="app-main">
        ${!isEnglishToRussian ? `
          <div>
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: center; align-items: center; gap: 20px; margin-bottom: var(--ui-gap);">
              <div style="font-size: var(--counter-font-size); font-weight: bold; color: #22c55e; visibility: ${showCounters && !isTestMode ? 'visible' : 'hidden'};">${sessionCorrectAnswers}</div>
              <p style="font-size: var(--task-font-size); font-weight: bold; margin: 0; line-height: 1.15;">${currentTask.ru}</p>
              <div style="font-size: var(--counter-font-size); font-weight: bold; color: #ef4444; visibility: ${showCounters && !isTestMode ? 'visible' : 'hidden'};">${sessionIncorrectAnswers}</div>
            </div>

            <input type="text" id="answer-display" inputmode="none" autocomplete="off" autocorrect="off" autocapitalize="off" spellcheck="false" value="${currentInput}" />
            <div id="feedback-msg" style="min-height: 22px; font-size: clamp(13px, 2dvh, 15px); font-weight: bold; color: ${lastFeedbackColor}; line-height: 1.15; margin-top: clamp(4px, 0.8dvh, 8px); visibility: ${showFeedback || lastFeedback ? 'visible' : 'hidden'};">${lastFeedback}</div>
          </div>
        ` : `
          <div>
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: center; align-items: center; gap: 20px; margin-bottom: var(--ui-gap);">
              <div style="font-size: var(--counter-font-size); font-weight: bold; color: #22c55e; visibility: ${showCounters && !isTestMode ? 'visible' : 'hidden'};">${sessionCorrectAnswers}</div>
              <p style="font-size: var(--task-font-size); font-weight: bold; margin: 0; line-height: 1.15;">${currentTask.en}</p>
              <div style="font-size: var(--counter-font-size); font-weight: bold; color: #ef4444; visibility: ${showCounters && !isTestMode ? 'visible' : 'hidden'};">${sessionIncorrectAnswers}</div>
            </div>

            <div id="feedback-msg" style="min-height: 22px; font-size: clamp(13px, 2dvh, 15px); font-weight: bold; margin-bottom: var(--ui-gap); color: ${lastFeedbackColor}; line-height: 1.15; visibility: ${showFeedback ? 'visible' : 'hidden'};">${lastFeedback}</div>
            <div id="options-container" style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: clamp(6px, 1dvh, 10px);">
              ${currentTask.options.map(opt => `
                <button class="option-btn" style="font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer; text-align: center; border-radius: 8px;">${opt}</button>
              `).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
        `}
      </div>

      <div class="app-footer">
        <div id="keyboard"></div>
      </div>
    `;

    renderTimerStatusBar({
      containerId: 'timer-status-bar-container',
      formattedTime: formatTime(secondsElapsed),
      showTimer,
      showSoundToggle,
      isSoundEnabled,
      showSettingsButton,
      onToggleSound: () => {
        isSoundEnabled = !isSoundEnabled;
        localStorage.setItem('rkai_sound_enabled', isSoundEnabled);
        if (!isSoundEnabled) {
          stopErrorSpeech();
        }
        renderTrainer();
      },
      onOpenSettings: () => {
        clearPendingAdvance();
        stopErrorSpeech();
        openSettingsModal({
          user: currentUser,
          currentLevel,
          onBeforeOpen: () => flushSessionTime(),
          onToggleEnglishSpeech: (enabled) => {
            isEnglishPhraseSpeechActive = Boolean(enabled);
          }
        });
      }
    });

    renderCategoryFilters({
      containerId: 'category-filters-container',
      currentLevel,
      activeCategories,
      isTestMode,
      onToggleCategory: handleToggleCategory
    });

    if (!isEnglishToRussian) {
      const inputEl = document.getElementById('answer-display');
      let refreshShift = () => {};

      if (inputEl) {
        applyCustomKeyboardInputGuards(inputEl);

        if (!isAdvancingAfterSpeech) {
          setCustomInputValueWithCaret(inputEl, currentInput, currentInput.length);
          preserveInputFocusAndCaret(inputEl, currentInput.length);
        }

        const handleCursorMove = () => {
          if (!isAdvancingAfterSpeech) {
            currentInput = inputEl.value;
            refreshShift();
          }
        };

        inputEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (!isAdvancingAfterSpeech) {
            const caret = typeof inputEl.selectionStart === 'number' ? inputEl.selectionStart : inputEl.value.length;
            setCustomInputValueWithCaret(inputEl, inputEl.value, caret);
            preserveInputFocusAndCaret(inputEl, caret);
            handleCursorMove();
          }
        });

        inputEl.addEventListener('touchend', () => {
          requestAnimationFrame(handleCursorMove);
        });

        inputEl.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
          if (e.key !== 'Tab' && e.key !== 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
          }
        });
      }

      const kbApi = setupKeyboard({
        level: currentLevel,
        getInputState: () => {
          if (!inputEl) {
            return { value: currentInput, selectionStart: currentInput.length, selectionEnd: currentInput.length };
          }
          const start = typeof inputEl.selectionStart === 'number' ? inputEl.selectionStart : inputEl.value.length;
          const end = typeof inputEl.selectionEnd === 'number' ? inputEl.selectionEnd : start;
          return {
            value: inputEl.value,
            selectionStart: start,
            selectionEnd: end
          };
        },
        onInput: (char) => {
          if (isAdvancingAfterSpeech || !inputEl) return;
          const start = typeof inputEl.selectionStart === 'number' ? inputEl.selectionStart : inputEl.value.length;
          const end = typeof inputEl.selectionEnd === 'number' ? inputEl.selectionEnd : start;
          const val = inputEl.value;

          if (val.length < MAX_INPUT_LENGTH) {
            const nextVal = val.substring(0, start) + char + val.substring(end);
            const nextCaret = start + 1;
            currentInput = nextVal;
            setCustomInputValueWithCaret(inputEl, nextVal, nextCaret);
            preserveInputFocusAndCaret(inputEl, nextCaret);
          }
        },
        onBackspace: () => {
          if (isAdvancingAfterSpeech || !inputEl) return;
          const start = typeof inputEl.selectionStart === 'number' ? inputEl.selectionStart : inputEl.value.length;
          const end = typeof inputEl.selectionEnd === 'number' ? inputEl.selectionEnd : start;
          const val = inputEl.value;

          if (start !== end) {
            const nextVal = val.substring(0, start) + val.substring(end);
            currentInput = nextVal;
            setCustomInputValueWithCaret(inputEl, nextVal, start);
            preserveInputFocusAndCaret(inputEl, start);
          } else if (start > 0) {
            const nextVal = val.substring(0, start - 1) + val.substring(start);
            const nextCaret = start - 1;
            currentInput = nextVal;
            setCustomInputValueWithCaret(inputEl, nextVal, nextCaret);
            preserveInputFocusAndCaret(inputEl, nextCaret);
          } else {
            currentInput = val;
            setCustomInputValueWithCaret(inputEl, val, 0);
            preserveInputFocusAndCaret(inputEl, 0);
          }
        },
        onSpace: () => {
          if (isAdvancingAfterSpeech || !inputEl) return;
          const start = typeof inputEl.selectionStart === 'number' ? inputEl.selectionStart : inputEl.value.length;
          const end = typeof inputEl.selectionEnd === 'number' ? inputEl.selectionEnd : start;
          const val = inputEl.value;

          if (val.length < MAX_INPUT_LENGTH) {
            const nextVal = val.substring(0, start) + " " + val.substring(end);
            const nextCaret = start + 1;
            currentInput = nextVal;
            setCustomInputValueWithCaret(inputEl, nextVal, nextCaret);
            preserveInputFocusAndCaret(inputEl, nextCaret);
          }
        },
        onOk: async () => {
          if (isAdvancingAfterSpeech) return;
          if (inputEl) currentInput = inputEl.value;
          const trimmedInput = currentInput.trim();

          if (!trimmedInput) {
            lastFeedback = "Сначала введите перевод";
            lastFeedbackColor = "#eab308";
            renderTrainer();
            return;
          }

          const isCorrect = trimmedInput.toLowerCase() === currentTask.en.toLowerCase();
          await handleAnswerResult(isCorrect, isCorrect ? trimmedInput : "");
        }
      });

      if (kbApi && typeof kbApi.refreshShiftState === 'function') {
        refreshShift = kbApi.refreshShiftState;
        refreshShift();
      }
    } else {
      setupOptions();
    }

    setupModals({ currentLevel });
    if (!isTestMode) startTimer();

    const applySoftUpdateBtn = document.getElementById('apply-soft-update-btn');
    if (applySoftUpdateBtn) {
      applySoftUpdateBtn.onclick = async () => {
        const versionToApply = availableUpdateVersion;
        availableUpdateVersion = null;
        clearPendingAdvance();
        stopErrorSpeech();
        renderTrainer();
        resetViewportPosition();
        flushSessionTime();
        await syncUserProgressToCloud();
        await applyPendingAppUpdate(versionToApply);
      };
    }

    const shareTopBtn = document.getElementById('share-top-btn');
    if (shareTopBtn) {
      shareTopBtn.onclick = () => {
        shareProjectContent({
          title: "Pareto English — самый эффективный тренажёр английского",
          text: buildProjectShareText(),
          url: SHARE_APP_URL
        });
      };
    }

    const startTestBtn = document.getElementById('start-test-btn');
    if (startTestBtn) {
      startTestBtn.onclick = () => {
        const testMins = Math.floor(gameConfig.testTimeSeconds / 60);
        const testSecs = gameConfig.testTimeSeconds % 60;
        const timeStr = `${testMins > 0 ? testMins + ' мин. ' : ''}${testSecs > 0 ? testSecs + ' сек.' : ''}`;

        if (confirm(`Вы переходите в режим тестирования на уровень ${currentLevel + 1}. Вам нужно ответить на ${gameConfig.testQuestionCount} вопросов за ${timeStr} (по схеме 7 рус-анг / 3 англ-рус). Начать?`)) {
          startTestMode();
        }
      };
    }

    const installBtn = document.getElementById('install-pwa-btn');
    if (installBtn) {
      installBtn.onclick = async () => {
        if (!deferredPrompt) return;
        deferredPrompt.prompt();
        const { outcome } = await deferredPrompt.userChoice;
        if (outcome === 'accepted') {
          console.log('Пользователь установил PWA');
        }
        deferredPrompt = null;
        renderTrainer();
      };
    }

    const adminBtn = document.getElementById('admin-panel-btn');
    if (adminBtn) {
      adminBtn.onclick = () => openAdminPanel();
    }

    const loginTriggerBtn = document.getElementById('login-trigger-btn');
    if (loginTriggerBtn) {
      loginTriggerBtn.onclick = async () => {
        try {
          await signInWithPopup(auth, googleProvider);
        } catch (error) {
          alert("Ошибка входа: " + error.message);
        }
      };
    }

    const logoutBtn = document.getElementById('logout-btn');
    if (logoutBtn) {
      logoutBtn.onclick = async () => {
        flushSessionTime();
        if (userUnsubscribe) {
          userUnsubscribe();
          userUnsubscribe = null;
        }
        resetAllLevelsAndCountersForLogout();
        renderTrainer();
        try {
          await signOut(auth);
        } catch (error) {
          alert("Ошибка выхода: " + error.message);
        }
      };
    }

    maybeShowStartupPresentation();
  }

  function setupOptions() {
    const optionButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.option-btn');
    optionButtons.forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        if (isAdvancingAfterSpeech) return;
        const isCorrect = btn.textContent.trim() === currentTask.correct;
        await handleAnswerResult(isCorrect);
      });
    });
  }
});

=== FILE: ./src/utils/speechFeedback.js ===
// src/utils/speechFeedback.js

const EN_PHRASE_SPEECH_STORAGE_KEY = 'rkai_en_phrase_speech_enabled';

/**
 * Возвращает состояние озвучки английских фраз (по умолчанию выключена).
 */
export function isEnglishPhraseSpeechEnabled() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !window.localStorage) {
    return false;
  }
  return localStorage.getItem(EN_PHRASE_SPEECH_STORAGE_KEY) === 'true';
}

/**
 * Сохраняет состояние озвучки английских фраз.
 */
export function setEnglishPhraseSpeechEnabled(enabled) {
  const nextValue = Boolean(enabled);
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.localStorage) {
    localStorage.setItem(EN_PHRASE_SPEECH_STORAGE_KEY, nextValue ? 'true' : 'false');
  }
  if (!nextValue) {
    stopErrorSpeech();
  }
  return nextValue;
}

function pickVoiceByLang(langPrefix, preferredKeywords = []) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('speechSynthesis' in window)) {
    return null;
  }

  const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices() || [];
  if (!voices.length) return null;

  const matchingVoices = voices.filter(
    (v) => v.lang && v.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(langPrefix.toLowerCase())
  );
  if (!matchingVoices.length) return null;

  // Ищем наиболее естественные голоса по ключевым словам (Google, Natural, Samantha и т.д.)
  for (const keyword of preferredKeywords) {
    const found = matchingVoices.find((v) =>
      v.name.toLowerCase().includes(keyword.toLowerCase())
    );
    if (found) return found;
  }

  return matchingVoices.find((v) => v.localService) || matchingVoices[0];
}

/**
 * Озвучивает шуточное сообщение при повторных ошибках (RU)
 */
export function speakErrorMessage(text, { enabled = true, rate = 1.02, pitch = 1.05 } = {}) {
  if (!enabled || !text) return;
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('speechSynthesis' in window)) return;

  try {
    const synth = window.speechSynthesis;
    if (synth.speaking || synth.pending) {
      synth.cancel();
    }

    const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(String(text).trim());
    utterance.lang = 'ru-RU';
    utterance.rate = rate;
    utterance.pitch = pitch;
    utterance.volume = 1;

    const voice = pickVoiceByLang('ru', ['google', 'milena', 'yuri', 'irina']);
    if (voice) {
      utterance.voice = voice;
    }

    synth.speak(utterance);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Ошибка синтеза русской речи:', err);
  }
}

/**
 * Озвучивает английское слово или фразу (EN)
 */
export function speakEnglishWord(text, { rate = 0.92, pitch = 1.0, onStart, onEnd } = {}) {
  if (!text) return;
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('speechSynthesis' in window)) return;

  try {
    const synth = window.speechSynthesis;
    if (synth.speaking || synth.pending) {
      synth.cancel();
    }

    const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(String(text).trim());
    utterance.lang = 'en-US';
    utterance.rate = rate;
    utterance.pitch = pitch;
    utterance.volume = 1;

    // Приоритет естественным американским/британским голосам
    const voice =
      pickVoiceByLang('en-us', ['google us english', 'natural', 'samantha', 'aria', 'jenny']) ||
      pickVoiceByLang('en-gb', ['google uk english', 'daniel', 'serena']) ||
      pickVoiceByLang('en', ['google', 'natural']);

    if (voice) {
      utterance.voice = voice;
    }

    if (typeof onStart === 'function') {
      utterance.onstart = onStart;
    }
    if (typeof onEnd === 'function') {
      utterance.onend = onEnd;
      utterance.onerror = onEnd;
    }

    synth.speak(utterance);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Ошибка синтеза английской речи:', err);
    if (typeof onEnd === 'function') onEnd();
  }
}

export function stopErrorSpeech() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('speechSynthesis' in window)) return;
  try {
    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  } catch (_) {}
}

=== FILE: ./src/utils/statsTracker.js ===
// src/utils/statsTracker.js
import { collection, doc, getDocs, setDoc, deleteDoc, increment, serverTimestamp } from "firebase/firestore";
import { db } from "../firebase.js";

const LOCAL_STATS_PREFIX = 'rkai_daily_stats_';
const LOCAL_COUNTERS_PREFIX = 'rkai_counters_';

export function getScopeKey(user) {
  return user && user.uid ? `user_${user.uid}` : 'guest';
}

function getLocalCountersKey(user) {
  return `${LOCAL_COUNTERS_PREFIX}${getScopeKey(user)}`;
}

function getLocalStatsKey(user) {
  return `${LOCAL_STATS_PREFIX}${getScopeKey(user)}`;
}

/**
 * Возвращает ключ даты в локальном часовом поясе: YYYY-MM-DD
 */
export function getLocalDateKey(date = new Date()) {
  const year = date.getFullYear();
  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
}

/**
 * Форматирует секунды в минуты или часы (без секунд)
 */
export function formatStatsDuration(totalSeconds) {
  const secs = Math.max(0, Math.round(totalSeconds || 0));
  if (secs === 0) return '0 мин';

  const totalMinutes = Math.round(secs / 60);
  if (totalMinutes < 1) return '< 1 мин';

  if (totalMinutes < 60) {
    return `${totalMinutes} мин`;
  }

  const hours = Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60);
  const mins = totalMinutes % 60;
  return mins > 0 ? `${hours} ч ${mins} мин` : `${hours} ч`;
}

/**
 * Загружает дневную статистику из localStorage для конкретного scope (user / guest)
 */
export function getLocalDailyStats(user = null) {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(getLocalStatsKey(user));
    if (!raw) return {};
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' ? parsed : {};
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("Ошибка чтения локальной статистики:", e);
    return {};
  }
}

function saveLocalDailyStats(user, statsMap) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(getLocalStatsKey(user), JSON.stringify(statsMap));
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("Ошибка сохранения локальной статистики:", e);
  }
}

/**
 * Единое хранилище счётчиков правильных и неправильных ответов (гость / пользователь)
 */
export function getLocalCounters(user) {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(getLocalCountersKey(user));
    if (!raw) return { correct: 0, incorrect: 0 };
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return {
      correct: Math.max(0, Number(parsed?.correct) || 0),
      incorrect: Math.max(0, Number(parsed?.incorrect) || 0)
    };
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("Ошибка чтения счётчиков:", e);
    return { correct: 0, incorrect: 0 };
  }
}

export function saveLocalCounters(user, { correct = 0, incorrect = 0 }) {
  try {
    const normalized = {
      correct: Math.max(0, Number(correct) || 0),
      incorrect: Math.max(0, Number(incorrect) || 0)
    };
    localStorage.setItem(getLocalCountersKey(user), JSON.stringify(normalized));
    return normalized;
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("Ошибка сохранения счётчиков:", e);
    return { correct: 0, incorrect: 0 };
  }
}

/**
 * Единая запись результата ответа:
 * обновляет счётчики правильных/неправильных ответов и дневную статистику выполненных заданий
 */
export async function recordAnswerAttempt({ user, isCorrect, countTowardCompletedTasks = true }) {
  const prev = getLocalCounters(user);
  const nextCounters = isCorrect
    ? { correct: prev.correct + 1, incorrect: prev.incorrect }
    : { correct: prev.correct, incorrect: prev.incorrect + 1 };

  saveLocalCounters(user, nextCounters);

  if (isCorrect && countTowardCompletedTasks) {
    await recordDailyProgress({ user, tasksDelta: 1, secondsDelta: 0 });
  }

  return nextCounters;
}

/**
 * Записывает прогресс за текущий день (выполненные задания и/или секунды) локально и в Firestore
 */
export async function recordDailyProgress({ user, tasksDelta = 0, secondsDelta = 0 }) {
  if (tasksDelta <= 0 && secondsDelta <= 0) return;

  const dateKey = getLocalDateKey();
  const localStats = getLocalDailyStats(user);
  const currentDay = localStats[dateKey] || { tasks: 0, seconds: 0 };

  localStats[dateKey] = {
    tasks: (currentDay.tasks || 0) + Math.max(0, tasksDelta),
    seconds: (currentDay.seconds || 0) + Math.max(0, secondsDelta)
  };
  saveLocalDailyStats(user, localStats);

  if (!user) return;

  try {
    const dayDocRef = doc(db, "users", user.uid, "dailyStats", dateKey);
    const payload = {
      date: dateKey,
      updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
    };
    if (tasksDelta > 0) payload.tasks = increment(tasksDelta);
    if (secondsDelta > 0) payload.seconds = increment(secondsDelta);

    await setDoc(dayDocRef, payload, { merge: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("Не удалось синхронизировать дневную статистику с Firestore:", err.message);
  }
}

/**
 * Загружает статистику пользователя из Firestore (если авторизован) или из localStorage
 */
export async function loadFullUserStats(user) {
  if (!user) {
    return { ...getLocalDailyStats(null) };
  }

  try {
    const statsColRef = collection(db, "users", user.uid, "dailyStats");
    const snapshot = await getDocs(statsColRef);

    const cloudMap = {};
    snapshot.forEach((docSnap) => {
      const data = docSnap.data();
      cloudMap[docSnap.id] = {
        tasks: Math.max(0, Number(data.tasks) || 0),
        seconds: Math.max(0, Number(data.seconds) || 0)
      };
    });

    saveLocalDailyStats(user, cloudMap);
    return cloudMap;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("Ошибка загрузки статистики из Firestore, используются локальные данные:", err.message);
    return { ...getLocalDailyStats(user) };
  }
}

/**
 * Полный сброс счётчиков и дневной статистики для указанного пользователя (например, из админки)
 */
export async function resetUserStatsAndCounters(uid) {
  try {
    localStorage.removeItem(`${LOCAL_COUNTERS_PREFIX}user_${uid}`);
    localStorage.removeItem(`${LOCAL_STATS_PREFIX}user_${uid}`);
    localStorage.removeItem('rkai_daily_stats');
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("Ошибка очистки локальных данных при сбросе:", e);
  }

  if (!uid) return;

  try {
    const statsColRef = collection(db, "users", uid, "dailyStats");
    const snapshot = await getDocs(statsColRef);
    const deletions = [];
    snapshot.forEach((docSnap) => {
      deletions.push(deleteDoc(docSnap.ref));
    });
    await Promise.all(deletions);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("Не удалось очистить dailyStats в Firestore:", err.message);
  }
}

=== FILE: ./src/utils/pwaManifestCompat.js ===
// src/utils/pwaManifestCompat.js
import { generateBrandedIconDataUrl } from './pwaBranding.js';

function checkImageExists(src) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    if (!src || typeof Image === 'undefined') {
      resolve(false);
      return;
    }
    const img = new Image();
    img.onload = () => resolve(img.naturalWidth > 0);
    img.onerror = () => resolve(false);
    img.src = src;
  });
}

/**
 * Обеспечивает корректную иконку для iOS (apple-touch-icon):
 * если /icon-180.png или /icon-192.png отсутствует, подставляет сгенерированную PNG-иконку.
 */
export async function syncPlatformIcons() {
  if (typeof document === 'undefined') return;

  const has180 = await checkImageExists('/icon-180.png');
  const has192 = await checkImageExists('/icon-192.png');

  let fallbackIconUrl = null;
  if (!has180 && !has192) {
    fallbackIconUrl = await generateBrandedIconDataUrl(192, '/icon-512.png');
  }

  const targetHref = has180
    ? '/icon-180.png'
    : has192
      ? '/icon-192.png'
      : fallbackIconUrl;

  if (!targetHref) return;

  let appleIcon = document.querySelector('link[rel="apple-touch-icon"]');
  if (!appleIcon) {
    appleIcon = document.createElement('link');
    appleIcon.setAttribute('rel', 'apple-touch-icon');
    document.head.appendChild(appleIcon);
  }
  appleIcon.setAttribute('href', targetHref);
}

=== FILE: ./src/utils/pwaBranding.js ===
// src/utils/pwaBranding.js
// Модуль брендинга PWA: формирует иконки и splash-изображения с крупным синим названием сайта

export const BRAND_NAME = 'Pareto English';
export const BRAND_SHORT_NAME = 'Pareto';
// Насыщенный, хорошо читаемый синий оттенок для названия сайта
export const BRAND_BLUE_PRIMARY = '#0284c7';
export const BRAND_BLUE_BRIGHT = '#38bdf8';
export const SPLASH_BG_COLOR = '#0f172a';
export const ICON_BG_COLOR = '#f8fafc';

function loadImage(src) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    if (!src || typeof Image === 'undefined') {
      resolve(null);
      return;
    }
    const img = new Image();
    img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    img.onload = () => resolve(img);
    img.onerror = () => resolve(null);
    img.src = src;
  });
}

function drawRoundedRect(ctx, x, y, width, height, radius) {
  const r = Math.min(radius, width / 2, height / 2);
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
  ctx.arcTo(x + width, y, x + width, y + height, r);
  ctx.arcTo(x + width, y + height, x, y + height, r);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y + height, x, y, r);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + width, y, r);
  ctx.closePath();
}

function drawFallbackMark(ctx, cx, cy, size, strokeColor = BRAND_BLUE_PRIMARY) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.strokeStyle = strokeColor;
  ctx.fillStyle = strokeColor;
  ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(4, size * 0.08);
  ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  ctx.lineJoin = 'round';

  const r = size * 0.42;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.stroke();

  ctx.font = `800 ${Math.round(size * 0.44)}px system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif`;
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  ctx.fillText('P', cx, cy + size * 0.02);
  ctx.restore();
}

/**
 * Генерирует квадратную иконку с логотипом и заметной синей подписью названия
 */
export async function generateBrandedIconDataUrl(size = 512, existingLogoSrc = '/icon-512.png') {
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = size;
  canvas.height = size;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  if (!ctx) return null;

  ctx.fillStyle = ICON_BG_COLOR;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);

  const logoImg = await loadImage(existingLogoSrc);
  const labelHeight = Math.round(size * 0.22);
  const logoAreaHeight = size - labelHeight - Math.round(size * 0.08);
  const markSize = Math.min(Math.round(size * 0.52), logoAreaHeight);
  const markCenterY = Math.round(size * 0.06) + markSize / 2;

  if (logoImg && logoImg.naturalWidth > 0) {
    ctx.drawImage(
      logoImg,
      (size - markSize) / 2,
      markCenterY - markSize / 2,
      markSize,
      markSize
    );
  } else {
    drawFallbackMark(ctx, size / 2, markCenterY, markSize, BRAND_BLUE_PRIMARY);
  }

  ctx.fillStyle = BRAND_BLUE_PRIMARY;
  ctx.font = `800 ${Math.round(size * 0.115)}px system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif`;
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  ctx.fillText(BRAND_SHORT_NAME, size / 2, size - labelHeight * 0.48, size * 0.9);

  return canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
}

/**
 * Генерирует полноэкранный splash-баннер для iOS с логотипом и крупным синим названием
 */
export async function generateIosSplashDataUrl(width, height, dpr = 2, existingLogoSrc = '/icon-512.png') {
  const pxW = Math.round(width * dpr);
  const pxH = Math.round(height * dpr);

  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = pxW;
  canvas.height = pxH;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  if (!ctx) return null;

  ctx.fillStyle = SPLASH_BG_COLOR;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, pxW, pxH);

  const minSide = Math.min(pxW, pxH);
  const cardSize = Math.round(minSide * 0.34);
  const cardX = (pxW - cardSize) / 2;
  const cardY = pxH * 0.42 - cardSize / 2;

  drawRoundedRect(ctx, cardX, cardY, cardSize, cardSize, cardSize * 0.22);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
  ctx.fill();

  const logoImg = await loadImage(existingLogoSrc);
  const pad = Math.round(cardSize * 0.14);

  if (logoImg && logoImg.naturalWidth > 0) {
    ctx.drawImage(logoImg, cardX + pad, cardY + pad, cardSize - pad * 2, cardSize - pad * 2);
  } else {
    drawFallbackMark(ctx, pxW / 2, cardY + cardSize / 2, cardSize - pad * 2, BRAND_BLUE_PRIMARY);
  }

  const titleSize = Math.max(28, Math.round(minSide * 0.075));
  ctx.fillStyle = BRAND_BLUE_BRIGHT;
  ctx.font = `800 ${titleSize}px system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif`;
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'top';
  ctx.fillText(BRAND_NAME, pxW / 2, cardY + cardSize + Math.round(minSide * 0.055), pxW * 0.9);

  return canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
}

/**
 * Подключает динамический apple-touch-startup-image под текущий экран устройства
 */
export async function applyDynamicSplashScreen() {
  if (typeof document === 'undefined' || typeof window === 'undefined') return;

  const width = window.screen?.width || window.innerWidth || 390;
  const height = window.screen?.height || window.innerHeight || 844;
  const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 2, 3);

  const splashUrl = await generateIosSplashDataUrl(width, height, dpr, '/icon-512.png');
  if (!splashUrl) return;

  let linkEl = document.querySelector('link[rel="apple-touch-startup-image"]');
  if (!linkEl) {
    linkEl = document.createElement('link');
    linkEl.setAttribute('rel', 'apple-touch-startup-image');
    document.head.appendChild(linkEl);
  }
  linkEl.setAttribute('href', splashUrl);
}

=== FILE: ./src/utils/overlayStack.js ===
// src/utils/overlayStack.js

const overlayStack = [];
let isPopstateListening = false;
let isHandlingPopstate = false;
let pendingHistoryBackCount = 0;

function ensurePopstateListener() {
  if (isPopstateListening || typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  isPopstateListening = true;

  window.addEventListener('popstate', () => {
    if (pendingHistoryBackCount > 0) {
      pendingHistoryBackCount--;
      return;
    }

    const topOverlay = overlayStack[overlayStack.length - 1];
    if (!topOverlay) return;

    isHandlingPopstate = true;
    try {
      overlayStack.pop();
      if (typeof topOverlay.onClose === 'function') {
        topOverlay.onClose();
      }
    } finally {
      isHandlingPopstate = false;
    }
  });
}

/**
 * Регистрирует открытое окно поверх стека и добавляет запись в историю браузера.
 * @param {string} id - Уникальный идентификатор окна (например, 'simple-hint' или 'intro-presentation').
 * @param {Function} onClose - Функция визуального закрытия окна без повторного вызова history.back().
 */
export function registerOverlayOpen(id, onClose) {
  ensurePopstateListener();

  const existingIndex = overlayStack.findIndex(item => item.id === id);
  if (existingIndex !== -1) {
    overlayStack.splice(existingIndex, 1);
  }

  overlayStack.push({ id, onClose });

  if (typeof history !== 'undefined' && typeof history.pushState === 'function') {
    history.pushState(
      { overlayId: id, stackDepth: overlayStack.length, ts: Date.now() },
      ''
    );
  }
}

/**
 * Закрывает окно по id (по умолчанию — верхнее).
 * Если закрытие вызвано не событием popstate, делает шаг назад в истории браузера.
 * @param {string} [id] - Идентификатор закрываемого окна. Если не передан, берётся верхнее.
 * @param {Function} [fallbackVisualClose] - Запасной колбэк, если окна нет в стеке.
 */
export function closeOverlayByUi(id, fallbackVisualClose) {
  if (isHandlingPopstate) return;

  let targetIndex = -1;
  if (id) {
    for (let i = overlayStack.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      if (overlayStack[i].id === id) {
        targetIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }
  } else if (overlayStack.length > 0) {
    targetIndex = overlayStack.length - 1;
  }

  let removedEntry = null;
  if (targetIndex !== -1) {
    [removedEntry] = overlayStack.splice(targetIndex, 1);
  }

  if (removedEntry && typeof removedEntry.onClose === 'function') {
    removedEntry.onClose();
  } else if (typeof fallbackVisualClose === 'function') {
    fallbackVisualClose();
  }

  if (
    removedEntry &&
    typeof history !== 'undefined' &&
    typeof history.back === 'function'
  ) {
    pendingHistoryBackCount++;
    history.back();
  }
}

/**
 * Проверяет, открыто ли сейчас конкретное окно (или любое окно, если id не передан).
 */
export function isOverlayOpen(id) {
  if (!id) return overlayStack.length > 0;
  return overlayStack.some(item => item.id === id);
}

=== FILE: ./src/utils/styleTheme.js ===
// src/utils/styleTheme.js

const GOLDEN_ANGLE = 137.508;
const BASE_HUE = 205; // Стартовый оттенок после 19-го уровня

function normalizeHue(hue) {
  return ((hue % 360) + 360) % 360;
}

/**
 * Генерирует мягкую светлую пастельную тему с высоким контрастом текста
 * для уровней стиля начиная с 20-го (и выше).
 */
export function getPastelThemeForLevel(styleLevel) {
  const stepIndex = Math.max(0, styleLevel - 20);
  const hue = Math.round(normalizeHue(BASE_HUE + stepIndex * GOLDEN_ANGLE));

  // Приглушённая насыщенность и высокая светлота — пастель без режущих глаз цветов
  const saturation = 28;
  const bgLightness = 94;
  const btnLightness = 88;
  const borderLightness = 72;
  const accentLightness = 38;
  const accentSat = 46;

  const radiusPx = Math.min(10, 8 + Math.floor(stepIndex / 3));

  return {
    hue,
    backgroundColor: `hsl(${hue}, ${saturation}%, ${bgLightness}%)`,
    color: '#0f172a',
    btnBg: `hsl(${hue}, ${saturation}%, ${btnLightness}%)`,
    btnBorder: `hsl(${hue}, ${Math.min(42, saturation + 10)}%, ${borderLightness}%)`,
    accent: `hsl(${hue}, ${accentSat}%, ${accentLightness}%)`,
    accentBg: `hsla(${hue}, ${accentSat}%, ${accentLightness}%, 0.14)`,
    boxShadow: `0 3px 8px hsla(${hue}, 35%, 25%, 0.08)`,
    borderRadius: `${radiusPx}px`
  };
}

/**
 * Применяет уровень стиля к document.body:
 * 0..19 — стили из evolution.css (витрина эволюции)
 * 20+   — бесконечная светлая пастельная палитра по золотому углу
 */
export function applyStyleLevelToBody(styleLevel) {
  const body = document.body;
  if (!body) return;

  body.setAttribute('data-level', String(styleLevel));

  const pastelVars = [
    'background-color',
    'color',
    '--border-radius',
    '--box-shadow',
    '--btn-bg',
    '--btn-border',
    '--accent',
    '--accent-bg'
  ];

  if (styleLevel < 20) {
    pastelVars.forEach((prop) => body.style.removeProperty(prop));
    return;
  }

  const theme = getPastelThemeForLevel(styleLevel);
  body.style.setProperty('background-color', theme.backgroundColor);
  body.style.setProperty('color', theme.color);
  body.style.setProperty('--border-radius', theme.borderRadius);
  body.style.setProperty('--box-shadow', theme.boxShadow);
  body.style.setProperty('--btn-bg', theme.btnBg);
  body.style.setProperty('--btn-border', theme.btnBorder);
  body.style.setProperty('--accent', theme.accent);
  body.style.setProperty('--accent-bg', theme.accentBg);
}

// src/utils/sessionReset.js
import { saveLocalCounters } from './statsTracker.js';
import { resetTaskPools } from '../data/tasks.js';

/**
 * Формирует чистое начальное состояние уровней, счётчиков и сессии
 * при выходе из аккаунта (переходе в гостевой режим).
 */
export function buildGuestResetState({ defaultTestSeconds = 120, resetGuestStorage = true } = {}) {
  if (resetGuestStorage) {
    saveLocalCounters(null, { correct: 0, incorrect: 0 });
  }

  resetTaskPools();

  return {
    currentUser: null,
    isAdminUser: false,
    currentLevel: 1,
    sessionCorrectAnswers: 0,
    sessionIncorrectAnswers: 0,
    totalCorrectAnswers: 0,
    totalIncorrectAnswers: 0,
    sessionIndex: 0,
    consecutiveErrors: 0,
    currentInput: '',
    lastFeedback: '',
    lastFeedbackColor: '',
    secondsElapsed: 0,
    lastSyncedSeconds: 0,
    isTestMode: false,
    testQuestionsAnswered: 0,
    testCorrectCount: 0,
    testSecondsLeft: Math.max(10, Number(defaultTestSeconds) || 120),
    activeCategories: ['statement']
  };
}

=== FILE: ./src/utils/appUpdate.js ===
// src/utils/appUpdate.js
import { doc, getDoc, setDoc, onSnapshot } from "firebase/firestore";
import { db } from "../firebase.js";

const SETTINGS_DOC_PATH = ["settings", "game"];
const LOCAL_APPLIED_VERSION_KEY = "rkai_applied_app_version";
const ATTEMPTED_VERSION_GUARD_KEY = "rkai_update_attempted_ver";

let pendingUpdateVersion = null;
let isApplyingUpdate = false;
let unsubscribeVersionListener = null;
const updateListeners = new Set();

function normalizeVersion(val, fallback = 1) {
  const num = Number(val);
  return Number.isFinite(num) && num > 0 ? Math.floor(num) : fallback;
}

export function getLocalAppliedVersion() {
  const raw = localStorage.getItem(LOCAL_APPLIED_VERSION_KEY);
  return normalizeVersion(raw, 1);
}

export function setLocalAppliedVersion(version) {
  const safeVer = normalizeVersion(version, 1);
  localStorage.setItem(LOCAL_APPLIED_VERSION_KEY, String(safeVer));
}

export function getPendingUpdateVersion() {
  return pendingUpdateVersion;
}

export function onUpdateAvailable(listener) {
  if (typeof listener !== "function") return () => {};
  updateListeners.add(listener);
  listener(pendingUpdateVersion);
  return () => updateListeners.delete(listener);
}

function notifyUpdateListeners() {
  updateListeners.forEach((fn) => {
    try {
      fn(pendingUpdateVersion);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn("Ошибка слушателя обновления:", err);
    }
  });
}

function registerAvailableVersion(remoteVersion) {
  const safeRemote = normalizeVersion(remoteVersion, 0);
  if (!safeRemote) return false;

  const localVersion = getLocalAppliedVersion();
  const attemptedInSession = normalizeVersion(
    sessionStorage.getItem(ATTEMPTED_VERSION_GUARD_KEY),
    0
  );

  // Если уже пытались обновиться до этой версии в текущей сессии — не триггерим повторно
  if (safeRemote <= localVersion || safeRemote === attemptedInSession) {
    return false;
  }

  if (pendingUpdateVersion !== safeRemote) {
    pendingUpdateVersion = safeRemote;
    notifyUpdateListeners();
  }
  return true;
}

export async function fetchRemoteAppVersion() {
  try {
    const ref = doc(db, ...SETTINGS_DOC_PATH);
    const snap = await getDoc(ref);
    if (snap.exists()) {
      const ver = normalizeVersion(snap.data().appVersion, 0);
      if (ver > 0) return ver;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("Не удалось получить версию приложения из Firestore:", err.message);
  }
  return getLocalAppliedVersion();
}

/**
 * Админ-действие: повышает версию приложения в Firestore для оповещения клиентов.
 */
export async function triggerForceAppUpdateForAllUsers() {
  const currentRemote = await fetchRemoteAppVersion();
  const currentLocal = getLocalAppliedVersion();
  const nextVersion = Math.max(currentRemote, currentLocal) + 1;

  const ref = doc(db, ...SETTINGS_DOC_PATH);
  await setDoc(
    ref,
    {
      appVersion: nextVersion,
      forceUpdateAt: Date.now()
    },
    { merge: true }
  );

  setLocalAppliedVersion(nextVersion);
  pendingUpdateVersion = null;
  notifyUpdateListeners();
  return nextVersion;
}

async function waitForWorkerActivation(reg, timeoutMs = 1500) {
  if (!reg) return;
  const worker = reg.installing || reg.waiting || reg.active;
  if (!worker) return;
  if (worker.state === "activated" && navigator.serviceWorker.controller) return;

  await new Promise((resolve) => {
    let done = false;
    const finish = () => {
      if (done) return;
      done = true;
      clearTimeout(timer);
      worker.removeEventListener("statechange", onStateChange);
      resolve();
    };
    const onStateChange = () => {
      if (worker.state === "activated") finish();
    };
    const timer = setTimeout(finish, timeoutMs);
    worker.addEventListener("statechange", onStateChange);
  });
}

/**
 * Мягко активирует новый Service Worker, очищает кэши файлов PWA и перезагружает страницу один раз.
 * Данные прогресса (localStorage / Firestore) не удаляются.
 */
export async function applyPendingAppUpdate(explicitVersion = null) {
  if (isApplyingUpdate) return;
  isApplyingUpdate = true;

  const targetVer = normalizeVersion(
    explicitVersion || pendingUpdateVersion || getLocalAppliedVersion(),
    1
  );

  // Фиксируем версию и одноразовую метку попытки до перезагрузки, чтобы избежать цикла
  setLocalAppliedVersion(targetVer);
  try {
    sessionStorage.setItem(ATTEMPTED_VERSION_GUARD_KEY, String(targetVer));
  } catch (_) {}

  pendingUpdateVersion = null;
  notifyUpdateListeners();

  try {
    if ("serviceWorker" in navigator) {
      const registrations = await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistrations();
      for (const reg of registrations) {
        await reg.update();
        if (reg.waiting) {
          reg.waiting.postMessage({ type: "SKIP_WAITING" });
        }
        await waitForWorkerActivation(reg);
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("Ошибка обновления Service Worker:", err);
  }

  try {
    if ("caches" in window) {
      const cacheKeys = await caches.keys();
      await Promise.all(cacheKeys.map((key) => caches.delete(key)));
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("Ошибка очистки Cache Storage:", err);
  }

  const url = new URL(window.location.href);
  url.searchParams.set("_v", String(targetVer));
  window.location.replace(url.toString());
}

/**
 * Совместимость со старым именем вызова: мягко фиксирует версию без принудительного срыва.
 */
export async function performHardPwaRefresh(targetVersion) {
  const safeVer = normalizeVersion(targetVersion, getLocalAppliedVersion());
  setLocalAppliedVersion(safeVer);
  pendingUpdateVersion = null;
  notifyUpdateListeners();
}

/**
 * Проверяет, появилась ли новая версия в Firestore (без принудительной перезагрузки).
 */
export async function checkAndApplyRemoteVersion(forceCheck = false) {
  if (isApplyingUpdate) return false;
  if (!forceCheck && document.visibilityState === "hidden") return false;

  const remoteVersion = await fetchRemoteAppVersion();
  return registerAvailableVersion(remoteVersion);
}

/**
 * Подписывается на изменения версии в реальном времени и показывает мягкое уведомление.
 */
export function startRemoteVersionWatcher() {
  if (unsubscribeVersionListener) return;

  try {
    const ref = doc(db, ...SETTINGS_DOC_PATH);
    unsubscribeVersionListener = onSnapshot(ref, (snap) => {
      if (!snap.exists() || isApplyingUpdate) return;
      const remoteVersion = normalizeVersion(snap.data().appVersion, 0);
      if (remoteVersion > 0) {
        registerAvailableVersion(remoteVersion);
      }
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("Ошибка подписки на версию приложения:", err);
  }
}

=== FILE: ./src/utils/platformCompat.js ===
// src/utils/platformCompat.js

/**
 * Определяет окружение браузера и платформы без поломки стандартного поведения Chrome.
 */
export function getPlatformInfo() {
  if (typeof navigator === 'undefined' || typeof window === 'undefined') {
    return {
      isYandex: false,
      isIos: false,
      isAndroid: false,
      isMobile: false,
      isStandalone: false
    };
  }

  const ua = navigator.userAgent || '';
  const isYandex = /YaBrowser|Yandex/i.test(ua);
  const isIos = /iPad|iPhone|iPod/i.test(ua) || (navigator.platform === 'MacIntel' && navigator.maxTouchPoints > 1);
  const isAndroid = /Android/i.test(ua);
  const isMobile = isIos || isAndroid || /Mobile|Mobi/i.test(ua);
  const isStandalone =
    window.matchMedia?.('(display-mode: standalone)')?.matches ||
    window.matchMedia?.('(display-mode: fullscreen)')?.matches ||
    Boolean(navigator.standalone);

  return {
    isYandex,
    isIos,
    isAndroid,
    isMobile,
    isStandalone
  };
}

/**
 * Обновляет CSS-переменную --app-vh с учётом visualViewport,
 * чтобы нижняя панель Яндекс Браузера и других браузеров не перекрывала интерфейс.
 */
export function syncViewportMetrics() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof document === 'undefined') return;

  const vv = window.visualViewport;
  const activeHeight = vv && vv.height > 0 ? vv.height : window.innerHeight;
  const vhUnitPx = Math.max(3.2, activeHeight * 0.01);

  document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--app-vh', `${vhUnitPx}px`);
}

/**
 * Запускает отслеживание размеров окна и visualViewport для кроссплатформенной адаптации.
 * Возвращает функцию очистки слушателей.
 */
export function initViewportAdaptation() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return () => {};

  syncViewportMetrics();

  const onResize = () => syncViewportMetrics();
  window.addEventListener('resize', onResize, { passive: true });
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange', onResize, { passive: true });

  const vv = window.visualViewport;
  if (vv) {
    vv.addEventListener('resize', onResize, { passive: true });
    vv.addEventListener('scroll', onResize, { passive: true });
  }

  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
    window.removeEventListener('orientationchange', onResize);
    if (vv) {
      vv.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
      vv.removeEventListener('scroll', onResize);
    }
  };
}

/**
 * Блокирует появление системной клавиатуры при работе со встроенной клавиатурой тренажёра,
 * сохраняя работу кастомного курсора (selectionStart / selectionEnd).
 */
export function applyCustomKeyboardInputGuards(inputEl) {
  if (!inputEl) return;

  const { isYandex, isMobile } = getPlatformInfo();

  inputEl.setAttribute('inputmode', 'none');
  inputEl.setAttribute('autocomplete', 'off');
  inputEl.setAttribute('autocorrect', 'off');
  inputEl.setAttribute('autocapitalize', 'off');
  inputEl.setAttribute('spellcheck', 'false');

  // Для мобильных устройств и особенно Яндекс Браузера readOnly надёжно подавляет системную клавиатуру,
  // не мешая программному управлению selectionStart / selectionEnd и значением value.
  if (isYandex || isMobile) {
    inputEl.readOnly = true;
  } else {
    inputEl.readOnly = false;
  }
}

/**
 * Безопасно задаёт значение и позицию курсора в поле ввода для кастомной клавиатуры.
 */
export function setCustomInputValueWithCaret(inputEl, nextValue, caretIndex) {
  if (!inputEl) return;

  const safeValue = String(nextValue ?? '');
  const safeCaret = Math.max(0, Math.min(Number(caretIndex) || 0, safeValue.length));

  inputEl.value = safeValue;
  try {
    inputEl.setSelectionRange(safeCaret, safeCaret);
  } catch (_) {
    try {
      inputEl.selectionStart = safeCaret;
      inputEl.selectionEnd = safeCaret;
    } catch (__) {}
  }
}

=== FILE: ./src/utils/share.js ===
// src/utils/share.js

export const SHARE_APP_URL = "https://eng.rkai.app";

export function buildProjectShareText() {
  return "Надоело забрасывать английский на старте? Pareto English — самый эффективный тренажёр английского: упрощает начальное изучение английского языка в 28 раз и доводит базу до автоматизма. Бесплатно:";
}

export function buildLevelUpShareText(nextLevel) {
  const safeLevel = Math.max(2, Number(nextLevel) || 2);
  return `Я прошёл тест и перешёл на Уровень ${safeLevel} в Pareto English — самом эффективном тренажёре английского! Упрощает начальное изучение английского языка в 28 раз и доводит базу до автоматизма. Бесплатно:`;
}

function formatShareMessage(text, url) {
  const cleanText = String(text || "").trim();
  const cleanUrl = String(url || "").trim();
  if (!cleanUrl) return cleanText;
  if (cleanText.includes(cleanUrl)) return cleanText;
  return cleanText ? `${cleanText}\n${cleanUrl}` : cleanUrl;
}

async function copyTextFallback(text) {
  if (navigator.clipboard && typeof navigator.clipboard.writeText === "function") {
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    return true;
  }

  const textarea = document.createElement("textarea");
  textarea.value = text;
  textarea.setAttribute("readonly", "");
  textarea.style.position = "fixed";
  textarea.style.opacity = "0";
  document.body.appendChild(textarea);
  textarea.select();

  let copied = false;
  try {
    copied = document.execCommand("copy");
  } catch (_) {
    copied = false;
  }

  document.body.removeChild(textarea);
  return copied;
}

/**
 * Открывает нативное меню «Поделиться» на устройстве,
 * а при его недоступности копирует текст со ссылкой в буфер обмена.
 */
export async function shareProjectContent({
  title = "Pareto English — самый эффективный тренажёр английского",
  text = buildProjectShareText(),
  url = SHARE_APP_URL
} = {}) {
  const fullShareMessage = formatShareMessage(text, url);

  if (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && typeof navigator.share === "function") {
    try {
      await navigator.share({
        title,
        text,
        url
      });
      return { status: "shared" };
    } catch (err) {
      if (err && err.name === "AbortError") {
        return { status: "cancelled" };
      }
    }
  }

  const copied = await copyTextFallback(fullShareMessage);
  if (copied) {
    alert(`Текст и ссылка скопированы в буфер обмена:\n\n${fullShareMessage}`);
    return { status: "copied" };
  }

  alert(`Скопируйте ссылку вручную:\n${url}`);
  return { status: "manual" };
}

=== FILE: ./src/counter.js ===
export function setupCounter(element) {
  let counter = 0
  const setCounter = (count) => {
    counter = count
    element.innerHTML = `Count is ${counter}`
  }
  element.addEventListener('click', () => setCounter(counter + 1))
  setCounter(0)
}
